# Py8n Build Worklog

Project: Py8n - the Python-native business operations platform
Stack: FastAPI + SQLAlchemy + Celery/Redis (backend) · Nuxt 3 + Vue Flow + Pinia + Tailwind (frontend)

---
Task ID: 1
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Recon environment + initialize fullstack env

Work Log:
- Ran init-fullstack.sh (Next.js scaffold created, dev.sh started next on :3000)
- Recon: Python 3.12.14 (venv /home/z/.venv), Node 24, Bun 1.3
- Preinstalled: fastapi 0.128, uvicorn, pydantic 2.12, Jinja2, APScheduler 3.11, cryptography, httpx, pytest, redis-py, fakeredis
- Installed: sqlalchemy 2.0.52, aiosqlite, celery 5.6.3, websockets
- NO redis-server / postgres / docker binaries in sandbox → design: SQLite + inline-executor fallback in sandbox; real Celery/Redis/Postgres via docker-compose for production
- Caddyfile :81 → generic XTransformPort query proxy to any localhost port (supports WS), default → :3000
- dev.sh contract: root package.json `dev` = main server :3000; mini-services/*/package.json with `dev` script auto-started

Stage Summary:
- Architecture: Nuxt3 :3000 (preview) · FastAPI :8000 (mini-services/api-backend) · LLM bridge :3010 (mini-services/llm-bridge, z-ai-web-dev-sdk)
- DB: SQLite (aiosqlite) w/ JSONB variant for PG; dispatcher pattern inline↔Celery; EventBus memory↔Redis pub/sub

Task ID: 12 (v3 feature wave - Loop engine + integrations)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Add Loop Over Items (per-batch body execution), Email/Slack integration nodes, UI support

Work Log:
- Loop engine (engine/nodes/loop.py + runner.py): loop_over_items node with loop/done handles; runner orchestrates body subgraph per batch via nested GraphRunner (hidden _batch_trigger virtual node re-sources loop-handle edges, seed context = parent node states + current batch so bodies can reference pre-loop nodes); body node runs appended with batch_index tag and broadcast live (WS events batch-tagged, lifecycle frames filtered); done output = {batches, batch_size, total_items, items/results} (items alias so aggregate/filter chain directly)
- Structural validation (runner.validate_loops, wired into validate_graph_document → 400 on save/import/run): body closure (no outside ancestors), done-collision, sibling-loop body overlap (nested loops allowed via ancestor check)
- Integrations (engine/nodes/integrations.py): email_send (SMTP via vault credential type smtp, asyncio.to_thread smtplib, STARTTLS) + slack_message (incoming webhook URL or generic bot-token credential → chat.postMessage) - both dry_run=true default with full preview output
- Jinja sandbox fix (templating.py): DictKeyFirstSandbox - dict KEY access now wins over same-named methods ({{ input.items }} previously returned dict.items builtin and blew up); regression-safe for method calls on dicts without that key
- Seed: "Batch Orders Digest - loop demo" (trigger → loop(batch 2) → per-batch revenue code → aggregate(result.revenue) → report) + idempotent seed top-up so older DBs get new demos on boot
- Registry: 18 public node types; _batch_trigger registered but hidden from definitions/palette
- Tests: tests/test_v3_features.py 9 tests (batches+aggregation, closure violation save+run, inherited pre-loop refs, nested loops, zero batches, email/slack dry-runs, credential errors) - suite 21/21 PASS · phase1_demo PASS · smoke_test extended (18 node defs, hidden-node leak check, seeded digest run w/ per-batch revenues [19.75,140.5,68.75,28.9] + total 257.9, bad-loop 400, email/slack dry-run chain, LIVE slack→Py8n-webhook loopback 200) - ALL PASS
- Frontend: Repeat/Mail/Slack palette+card icons, SMTP credential form (host/port/user/pass/TLS, per-field secret flag), ExecutionsDrawer batch badges (batch N, sky chip) + stable keys for repeated node runs, NodeRun.batch_index type
- Ops: backend restarted via llm-bridge /_spawn (token-gated), cleaned leftover tmp workflows from earlier interrupted smoke run
- Browser E2E: loop node shows Loop/Done ports on canvas, config panel schema form (items_path), UI Run success 4ms with per-batch drawer entries + batch badges, palette search finds all 3 new nodes, no console errors; screenshots download/e2e-v3-loop-editor.png, e2e-v3-live-run.png, e2e-v3-loop-config.png

Stage Summary:
- Py8n v3: 18 node types, true loop support (per-batch execution, nested loops, closure guardrails), email/slack integrations with vault credentials + dry-run safety - all suites + browser verified

Task ID: 14 (v5 feature wave - Wait for Resume / human-in-the-loop)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Suspend/resume engine (approval-style pauses), resume API + UI, race hardening

Work Log:
- New node engine/nodes/wait.py: wait_for_resume (logic category, violet, pauses_execution marker, params resume_hint + pass_through, waiting_output() builder with method/URL/token)
- Runner: _suspend() pauses the run (context.register waiting, node_runs record, node_finished/execution_finished events, status=waiting) and returns resume info + full resume_state (node_states + active_edges); constructor resume_state arg rehydrates a second-pass runner (seeded states, recomputed active edges, Jinja context via inherit_node_states, prior_node_runs prepended); resume pass completes the paused node with the resume payload then continues downstream (already-run nodes skipped)
- Guards: validate_loops rejects wait nodes inside loop bodies; execute_workflow (subflow) raises on nested waiting status
- Executor: execute_workflow persists waiting rows (finished_at NULL) with context_snapshot.py8n_resume = {token, node_id, node_states, active_edges}; new resume_workflow() validates token (LookupError→404 / ValueError→409 / PermissionError→403), flips row to running SYNCHRONOUSLY before 202 (pollers never see stale waiting), then background _finish updates status/duration(first+second pass)/node_runs and drops py8n_resume (token single-use)
- API: POST /executions/{id}/resume {token, payload} → 202 {execution_id, resume_node}; GET /executions/{id} exposes resume {method,url,token,node_id} while waiting; schemas.ResumeRequest
- Race hardening (found via smoke flake): FastAPI yield-dependency commits run AFTER the response - create/update/delete/import/duplicate workflow endpoints now commit explicitly before returning (create→immediate run 404 race)
- Tests: tests/test_v5_features.py 7 tests (suspend shape, resume w/ pre-wait template context, skipped-wait no-pause, pass_through, loop-body rejection, API roundtrip w/ 404/403/409 + rerun-of-waiting + single-use token) - suite 30/30 PASS
- Smoke: v5 section (suspend, guards, resume to success w/ payload propagation, token 409) - ALL PASS; node count assertion 18→19
- Frontend: types (waiting statuses + ResumeInfo), palette 'pause-circle' icon, store.resumeExecution, /executions page (Waiting chip/filter, violet row border, Resume panel: hint + copyable POST URL + payload JSON textarea + Resume button w/ error handling), editor drawer + canvas violet waiting ring/status
- Seeded demo "Approval Gate - human-in-the-loop demo" via API
- Browser E2E: run from editor → drawer "waiting" + violet pulsing canvas ring → /executions Waiting(1) chip → detail Resume panel shows hint/URL/payload box → resume {"approved":true,"note":"Approved by CL-7 policy"} → same exec id success, node runs [waiting, success], verdict reimbursable=true, palette lists node, zero console errors; screenshots download/e2e-v5-editor-waiting.png + e2e-v5-resumed.png

Stage Summary:
- Py8n v5: 19 node types incl. Wait for Resume - true human-in-the-loop (persist + token-gated resume, same-execution continuation), commit-race hardened; 30/30 pytest + smoke + browser verified

Task ID: 16 (v7 feature wave - Automation lifecycle: schedules & activation)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Schedule introspection (next-fire previews), global schedules view, activate/deactivate endpoints, cron validation

Work Log:
- Scheduler service: validate_schedule_params (raises on bad crontab/non-int interval), describe_schedule (human summary: "cron 0 9 * * 1-5" / "every 15m" / "hourly"), next_fire_times (5 ISO-UTC previews), schedule_entries_for_graph (per schedule_trigger node: identity + summary + previews + error)
- APScheduler gotcha: get_next_fire_time(previous, now) computes min(now, previous+1µs) for CronTrigger → passing a future previous re-derives the SAME slot (all previews identical). Fix: walk the future by advancing now = nxt + 1µs with previous=None
- API: GET /workflows/{id}/schedule (schedules + next_run_at, null while paused); POST /workflows/{id}/activate (pre-flight: 400 "Cannot activate - schedule node 'X': ..." on unschedulable params) / deactivate (both explicit-commit + resync, return WorkflowScheduleOut); save-time cron validation on create/update (400, kills the silent-never-fires failure mode); WorkflowListItem += schedule_summary/next_run_at; new GET /schedules global router (active+healthy first sorted by next fire, invalid, paused last) registered in main.py; version bumped 1.0.0 → 1.7.0
- Tests: tests/test_v7_features.py 4 tests (ascending fire previews + 404, activate/deactivate roundtrip + list fields, save/activate cron rejection incl. DB-planted bad row pre-flight + non-int interval, global view ordering + names) - suite 38/38 PASS; smoke v7 section (previews advance, activate → next_run_at on list, global view, bad-cron 400, deactivate) - ALL PASS; phase1_demo PASS

Next-up, per-row mode chip + mono summary + next/then fire previews, Activate/Pause toggle with re-sort, broken-row styling, 30s auto-refresh, empty state); dashboard: Schedules nav link, card schedule line ("cron … · next in 15m / · paused"), toggle error alerting; editor: schedule pill next to triggers toggle (live green "every 24h · next in 1d" w/ upcoming-runs tooltip, paused grey, broken red), toggle toasts, schedule info refreshed on load/save

Task ID: 17 (v8 feature wave - Run control & failure routing)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Node disable (pass-through), execution cancel (hard+cooperative), error-workflow routing

Work Log:
- Context: session resumed with stale summary - worklog showed v5/v6/v7 already delivered (Task 14-16, 38/38 pytest). "proceed" authorized next wave = v8
- Node disable: NodeSpec.disabled flag; runner._pass_through_disabled records skipped + passes active input through as main output; _gather_active_inputs now accepts "skipped" states (payload None still filters); _pick_trigger skips disabled triggers (400 "All trigger nodes are disabled" when none left); works inside loop bodies via nested runner for free
- Cancel: POST /executions/{id}/cancel (202; 404 unknown; 409 not-running). Two mechanisms in executor: _cancel_flags[exec_id] cooperative Event (runner checks before each node → status=cancelled, completed nodes kept) + _running_tasks[exec_id] hard task.cancel() (aborts at next await point - long delay nodes stop instantly); execute_workflow catches CancelledError, finalises row (status=cancelled, node_runs so far) and swallows; dispatch_inline + resume _finish register tasks with id-keyed done callbacks
- Error workflow routing: workflows.error_workflow_id (String36, nullable) + init_db lightweight column migration (create_all doesn't add missing COLUMNS - dev SQLite needed manual ALTER, now idempotent); executor dispatches handler on unhandled error with structured payload {execution_id, workflow_id, workflow_name, error, failed_nodes[{node_id,node_name,error}]}; guards: trigger_type=="error" runs never re-route (no infinite loops), self-binding rejected at API (400), handler existence validated on create/update (400) + re-checked pre-dispatch; workflow PUT tri-state binding (omitted=untouched, ""=clear, str=bind); list resolves error_workflow_name (batch, no N+1)
- Template gotcha: handler graphs must read the error payload via {{ execution.trigger_payload.* }} - there is no bare "trigger" Jinja var (caught by v8 test)
- Version 1.7.0 → 1.8.0
- Tests: tests/test_v8_features.py 7 tests (disable pass-through, all-triggers-disabled, cancel-event engine test, error dispatch payload end-to-end, no-recursion guard, API cancel roundtrip + 404/409 guards, binding validation + list name + tri-state) - suite 45/45 PASS
- Smoke: v8 section (disabled node bypasses 1/0 with payload intact, cancel mid-run → cancelled + next node never ran + re-cancel 409, error handler dispatched with ZeroDivision payload + binding guards) - ALL PASS
- Frontend: types (NodeSpec.disabled, Workflow.error_workflow_id, cancelled status, WorkflowListItem error fields); store (workflows/loadWorkflows, setErrorWorkflow, cancelExecution/cancelling); ConfigPanel Disabled toggle (amber, ban icon) in node header; PNodeCard dashed+dimmed when disabled (borderTop #a16207, Ban badge); editor header "On error: stop / → <workflow>" shield selector (rose when bound, toast on change, self/unknown 400 surfaced); canvasToGraph now serializes disabled; executions page Cancel stop-button on running rows, Cancelled chip + Ban icon + zinc styling; dashboard card "on error → <name>" line
- Demo fixtures: "Nightly CRM Sync - v8 error demo" (fails → bound handler), "Ops Alert - error handler (v8)", "Slow Import - v8 cancel demo" (60s delay)
- Browser E2E: dashboard shows on-error line; editor selector shows bound state with all workflows; ConfigPanel toggle → node dashed+0.5 opacity (computed styles verified); executions Cancel click → Running 0/Cancelled 1 within 1.5s (hard cancel), row shows "Cancelled by user"; Ops Alert error-triggered run detail shows trigger payload; zero console errors; screenshots download/e2e-v8-{dashboard-onerror,editor-errorwf,node-disabled,cancel-button,cancelled-row,error-handler-run}.png

Stage Summary:
- Py8n v8: full run control & failure routing - nodes can be disabled (pass-through), running executions cancelable (hard + cooperative), unhandled failures route to bound error workflows with structured payloads; 45/45 pytest + smoke + browser verified, backend live at 1.8.0

Task ID: 22 (v13 feature wave - Workflow version history & restore)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Safety net for the editor - automatic content snapshots, bounded history, one-click restore from the canvas header

Work Log:
- Context: "awesome, proceed" after v12 → picked versioning from candidates (editors could lose work: Save overwrites with no undo)
- Backend: WorkflowVersion model in models.py (workflow_versions table: per-workflow monotonic version int, name/description/graph/tags/node_count, FK CASCADE + ORM relationship both sides so deletes cascade in Python like executions); services/versions.py owns the write path - next_version(), snapshot_workflow_version() (insert + prune beyond MAX_VERSIONS=20 on every snapshot, unbounded growth impossible)
- Snapshot policy: content only (graph / name / description) - create, import, duplicate, template-use all snapshot v1; every PUT touching content keys snapshots the post-state; tags / is_active / error_workflow_id deliberately DON'T pollute history; restore = apply snapshot content + snapshot again (append-only - nothing destroyed, redo = restore the formerly-current version)
- API: GET /workflows/{id}/versions (desc list, is_current flag, max_versions), GET .../versions/{version} (full graph detail), POST .../versions/{version}/restore (200 WorkflowOut); 404 unknown workflow/version; version bump 1.12.0 → 1.13.0
- Tests: tests/test_v13_features.py 2 tests (lifecycle: v1 on create, v2 on graph save, NO snapshot for tags/error-binding, v3 on rename, detail keeps original graph, restore→v4 append + tags survive, 4×404 guards; cap: 23 versions ever → 20 kept = versions 4..23, delete cascades to zero orphan rows) - suite 56/56 PASS
- Smoke: v13 section (v1 on create, org-change no-snapshot, restore v1→v3 append, tags survive, 25 total → 20 kept [6..25], 404 guards) - ALL PASS
- Frontend: types (WorkflowVersionSummary/List/Detail); store (versions, loadVersions, restoreVersion); editor header History button → modal (newest-first rows, v# chip, name + time + node count, orange CURRENT row, Restore per row with confirm dialog, footer explainer, cap counter); restore re-renders canvas via graphToCanvas + fitView + toast; sidebar footer v1.13
- E2E gotchas (repeat patterns): live dev DB needs a backend restart for create_all to add the new TABLE (in-process tests don't run lifespan) - same as v12's column wave; pre-v13 workflows honestly show the modal's empty state until their first save, then history accumulates
- Browser E2E: Quickstart (pre-v13, empty state shown) → 2 API saves → modal shows v2*/v1 → Restore v1 → confirm dialog accepted → rows [v3*, v2, v1], server-side versions verified [(3,True),(2,False),(1,False)] with per-version descriptions, canvas re-rendered, demo description restored afterwards; zero console errors
- Screenshots: download/e2e-v13-history-restored.png

Stage Summary:
- Py8n v13: editor gets a real safety net - every content save snapshots automatically (20-version rolling window), history modal with one-click restore that never destroys anything (restore lands as a new version); 56/56 pytest + smoke + browser verified, backend live at 1.13.0

Task ID: 24 (v15 feature wave - Environment variables)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Global template variables - CRUD + secret vault semantics + {{ env.KEY }} resolution in every node field

Work Log:
- Context: "proceed" #7. Recon FIRST (lesson held): candidate #2 "Execute Workflow node" was ALREADY implemented in an early wave (app/engine/nodes/subflow.py - payload templating, MAX_DEPTH=3 recursion guard, self-call guard, wait_for_completion, wait-node-inside-subflow error). Picked the remaining candidate: environment variables
- Backend: EnvVariable model (env_variables table; key String(64) unique-indexed, value_encrypted Text, is_secret, description, timestamps); crypto.py gained encrypt_value/decrypt_value (values ALWAYS Fernet-encrypted at rest - uniform path, no plaintext in DB); services/env_vars.py load_env_map() (decrypt-on-load, rotated-key rows degrade to "" + warning instead of failing unrelated runs)
- API app/api/env_vars.py: GET list (secret values masked to null) / POST 201 (400 invalid key, 409 dup case-insensitive via func.upper match) / GET {id} edit detail / PUT (value __keep__ marker preserves stored secret; is_secret flip unmasks the KEPT value) / DELETE 204. Key design: stored EXACTLY as typed (validated ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$, trimmed) - Jinja dict access is case-sensitive so forced UPPER normalization silently broke template references (caught by the engine test: template {{ env.v15_x_1de6a5ca }} vs stored V15_X_1DE6A5CA → unresolved). Explicit commit on all writes (v4 lesson)
- Engine: ExecutionContext.env_vars field exposed as "env" root in as_jinja_context(); GraphRunner gains env_vars kwarg - None = load once per run() via load_env_map; loop bodies receive the already-loaded map (no per-batch re-query); sub-workflows load fresh (depth ≤ 3). env deliberately EXCLUDED from context snapshot() - execution logs never carry an env dump
- Tests: tests/test_v15_features.py 2 tests (CRUD+masking+409 exact&case-variant+400 bad keys+__keep__+unmask-flip-reveals-kept+404s; engine: set_variable resolves plain/secret/default-filter-for-missing through {{ env.* }}, context_snapshot has no "env" key) - suite 58/58 PASS. Test-harness gotcha repeated: drain background tasks BEFORE asserting execution status (v11 pattern), else the run is read mid-flight and the first loop's shutdown cancels the committing task (transient "database is locked")
- Smoke: v15 section (create plain+secret → dup 409 → workflow code node resolves {{ env.* }} incl. secret → snapshot env-free → unmask flip reveals kept value → deletes) - ALL PASS; v14 health assert made forward-compatible (>= 1.14)
- Frontend: types EnvVariable; store envVars/loadEnvVars/create/update/delete (key-sorted insert); pages/env-vars/index.vue - sticky header w/ {{ env.KEY }} hint, stats strip (total/secrets/syntax), search, rows (mono key + secret badge + masked •••••••• or plaintext + description + updated), create/edit modal (key frozen when editing; secret value field write-only w/ "Leave blank to keep the current value"; secret toggle; 400/409 error banner), confirm-guarded delete, honest empty state; AppSidebar Variables (Variable icon) after Credentials, footer v1.15; CommandPalette Variables nav item (keywords env/environment/globals/config)
- Vue gotcha: raw {{ env.KEY }} strings inside template interpolations break compilation (parser closes at the first }}) - snippet rendered via a script constant ENV_SNIPPET instead
- Restart-first pattern (v12/v13 lesson): backend restarted BEFORE pytest so init_db created the new table; leftover rows from failed test attempts swept before the green run
- Browser E2E: empty state → create plaintext var (modal, disabled create until key) → create secret var (toggle) → masked •••••••• in list → edit modal: plaintext prefilled / secret blank+placeholder → UI-created vars resolved in a REAL run via scripts/e2e_v15_env_check.py (base URL + flag + secret length w/o exposure) → Ctrl+K "variables" → /env-vars → UI delete w/ confirm → empty state; sidebar shows 7 nav items, footer v1.15; dashboard regression clean; zero console errors
- Screenshots: download/e2e-v15-{env-vars,env-vars-collapsed}.png

Stage Summary:
- Py8n v15: workflows share configuration via global environment variables - {{ env.KEY }} resolves in any node field, values Fernet-encrypted at rest, secrets write-only (never echoed), engine loads the map once per run and keeps it out of execution logs; 58/58 pytest + smoke + browser verified, backend live at 1.15.0

Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: GitHub publication (Task 27 follow-through) + canvas undo/redo + Ctrl+C/V/D node clipboard

Work Log:
- (Task 27) Pushed Py8n to github.com/Omerhrr/py8n - see entry above
- Context: user said "push to GitHub first, then proceed to the next wave"; recon (worklog + API routes) showed v15/v16/v17 all delivered; import/export endpoints + UI ALREADY existed; genuine remaining gap = editor history/clipboard (n8n core ergonomics)
- Composables/useGraphHistory.ts: snapshot-based history (JSON {nodes,edges} stack, cap 80, redo-branch truncation, dedup-vs-top, suspended guard during apply); page keeps ONE instance per editor
- KEY BUG + FIX: Vue Flow's v-model write-back is DEFERRED - synchronous historyCommit() right after addNodes() read the OLD graph (identical snapshot → commit no-op → Undo stayed disabled). Fix: coalesced deferred commit (40ms timer) + flush/cancel logic in undo/redo (pending commit cancelled, nextTick re-commit dedups against the reverted state). Lesson: in Vue Flow, mutations via useVueFlow helpers (addNodes/addEdges) need nextTick before reading v-model refs - same family as the existing graphToCanvas+await nextTick pattern
- Tooling gotcha: MultiEdit was NOT atomic this time - edit 1 of 2 applied, edit 2 failed, leaving applySnapshot deleted while still referenced. Always re-read the file after a MultiEdit failure before re-attempting
- Commit points wired: addNode, connect, delete node/edge, drag-stop, updateParam, updateSettings, toggleDisabled, updatePinned, renameNode; history.reset on load AND on version restore (fresh timeline); paste = new ids (p_ prefix) + "+48/+48" offset cascade + internal edges remapped + n8n-style "name copy" suffix; Ctrl+D duplicates WITHOUT clobbering the clipboard
- Keyboard (window handler, input/textarea/select guarded): Ctrl+Z undo · Ctrl+Shift+Z / Ctrl+Y redo · Ctrl+C copy · Ctrl+V paste · Ctrl+D duplicate (joins existing Delete + Ctrl+S); toolbar gains Undo2/Redo2 icon buttons with disabled states bound to canUndo/canRedo computeds
- pytest 62/62 PASS (backend untouched; version 1.17.0 → 1.18.0, restart-first pattern held) · smoke ALL PASS · backend live at 1.18.0
- Browser E2E: undo/redo buttons render disabled on load → add HTTP node via palette (3→4, Undo enables) → Ctrl+Z 4→3 → Ctrl+Shift+Z 3→4 → Ctrl+Z 4→3 → Ctrl+Y 3→4 → select Doubler → Ctrl+C/V (4 nodes, "Doubler copy") → Ctrl+D (5) → Ctrl+S: server graph = 4 nodes w/ pasted id p_* type code + code param PRESERVED → Ctrl+Z → Ctrl+S: server back to 3 (undo+save round-trip integrity) → screenshots ×2 → zero console errors
- Screenshots: download/e2e-v18-{editor-paste,editor-final}.png

Stage Summary:
- Py8n v18: the canvas is now a real editor - snapshot undo/redo (Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Shift+Z / Ctrl+Y + toolbar) across structure, drag, params, settings, pins and renames, plus node clipboard (Ctrl+C/V/D) with internal-edge remapping and copy-name suffix; version restore resets the timeline; 62/62 pytest + smoke + browser verified, backend live at 1.18.0

Task ID: 36 (v26 feature wave - chat progress streaming over SSE)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Live execution feedback in the chat panel (node chips + typed replies)

Work Log:
- Context: session resumed; worklog showed v25 (Task 35) fully delivered, 93/93 pytest, backend 1.25.0, commit 8165fe0. Recon: tags exist (v12), retry exists (full-run), NO streaming anywhere. Tested the bridge with stream:true - it IGNORES it and returns a single JSON completion (glm-4-plus), so true token streaming is impossible today. Wave pivoted to the next best thing: live EXECUTION progress streaming to the chat (replaces the static "workflow is running…" bubble) + client-side typewriter reveal
- Backend (app/api/chat.py): POST /chat/{id}/stream - same guards (404/409/422 as plain JSON BEFORE the stream), then text/event-stream with frames: start {execution_id, session_id} -> node* (node_started => status running; node_finished => status+duration_ms+error) -> done {reply, output, via?} | error | timeout. ORDERING FIX: the bus pump task must be created BEFORE the flow task (tasks run in creation order; bus.subscribe only registers its queue on the first __anext__, which happens when the pump first runs) - first draft subscribed a queue manually into bus._subscribers AND pumped (double-subscribe + not Redis-bus-safe); rewrote to a single pump task using the official subscribe() generator
- respond_node mode: the responder's answer RACES the bus events inside the stream loop (asyncio.wait FIRST_COMPLETED over waiter.wait() vs queue.get(), cancelling losers) - responder wins => done frame with the custom body + "via": "respond_node", stream ends while the flow continues in background; execution_finished first => if responder already answered use its body, else error frame "finished without calling a Respond to Webhook node". Reply extraction from the finished event: it carries node_runs (NOT output) - last node_run's output feeds _extract_reply (first draft read event.output => empty reply)
- timeout: bounded by webhook_wait_seconds => timeout frame, flow task NOT cancelled (keeps running, records normally - nicer than the webhook last_node cancel path); CancelledError-safe via finally pump_task.cancel()
- tests/test_v26_features.py (5): happy path (6 node frames = 3 nodes x started/finished in topological order, durations ints, done reply "final: hello stream", execution trigger_type=chat; httpx client.stream + aiter_bytes - GOTCHA: aiter_bytes is an async generator, a stray `await` on it explodes); guards (404/409/409/422 all plain JSON, no event-stream content-type); respond_node custom body in done; error frame ([ChatPolicy] stop_and_error message surfaces); timeout (settings.webhook_wait_seconds monkeypatched to 1, delay node 4s => timeout frame, then poll executions until the background run records success). Suite 98/98
- Smoke v26 section (live 1.26.0): raw urllib SSE read (req() has no streaming), frames parsed => start, 4 node frames (2 nodes x started/finished), done reply "Echo: hello stream", execution_id round-trip. GOTCHA: BASE already includes /api/v1 (first draft built .../api/v1/api/v1/...). ALL PASS
- Frontend ChatPanel.vue rewritten: fetch() + ReadableStream reader (NOT EventSource - POST needed), buffer split on \n\n, event/data line parsing; LIVE step chips (one per node: spinner running / check success / x error + duration seconds) in a running bubble; done => assistant bubble pushed empty and typed out (typewriter 3 chars/18ms, cancels previous); error/timeout frames => amber error bubbles; guards JSON parsed into the same error bubble (inactive hint preserved); stream-ended-without-terminal fallback => plain POST /chat/{id}; network-level failure => fallback then error bubble. Session id + New conversation semantics unchanged; composer hint "live progress stream"
- E2E (browser): v25 Support Bot still active; chat panel open, sent "Stream test: my lucky number is seven." - chips appeared LIVE (Chat Trigger 0.00s, Support Agent 0.46s - model is fast so mid-run capture is tight) and the reply typed out; follow-up "What is my lucky number?" => "Your lucky number is seven." (same-session recall through the STREAM endpoint - agent memory + streaming verified together); zero console errors

- ENGINE (app/services/rules.py NEW): RULE_OPS (eq/ne/gt/gte/lt/lte/contains/not_contains/starts_with/ends_with/empty/not_empty), events create|update|always, actions block|warn|set; eval_condition is an {"all": [...]} conjunction (missing when → always matches); _num loose numeric coercion (bools NOT numbers, "" / None → empty); RuleBlocked(ValueError) surfaces as 400; eval_formula parses with ast and allows ONLY BinOp(+ - * / % **)/UnaryOp(±)/numeric Constant/Name - no calls/attrs/imports; non-numeric field or unknown name → ValueError (runtime: set skipped, submitted value kept); validate_rules (dup ids, unknown op/action/event, clause fields in schema, valueless ops, set needs value XOR formula, formula names ∈ schema, cap 50 → 400); apply_rules returns (record, warnings), block short-circuits, set values cast via _cast_set per column dtype (float→int for integer cols); dry_run() same evaluation without raising → {matches, warnings, blocked, record} for the builder test endpoint

- API (app/api/apps.py): GET/PUT /apps/{ref}/rules - PUT works on PUBLISHED apps (rules are governance; the layout lock stays); POST /apps/{ref}/rules/test dry-run (no writes); GET /apps/{slug}/form descriptor (published + form component required: draft 404, no-form 409) returns normalized fields; POST /apps/{slug}/form-submit anonymous collection through the SAME pipeline; records POST/PATCH responses now carry warnings; _form_comp picks the first form component; schemas RulesPut (cap owned by service with 400 - first draft capped in Pydantic → 422, test caught) + RulesTestIn; config.py version 1.30.0; node count UNCHANGED at 36 (app-platform wave)

Task ID: 42 (v32 feature wave - Document AI: documents in, datasets out)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: Document extraction as a first-class capability - pdfplumber PDF (text + ruled tables), tesseract OCR, docx/xlsx/csv/json engines, /documents API (extract + to-dataset), document_extract node (36→37), Document AI page

Work Log:
- Context: user said "proceed" after v31; original roadmap's next capability = Document extraction. Recon: worklog Task 41 confirmed, backend 1.31.0 clean at 758841f. Dependency check found the ENTIRE stack already present in the venv - tesseract 5.5.0 binary + pytesseract 0.3.13 + pypdf + PDFPLUMBER 0.11.9 (ruled-table extraction) + python-docx + reportlab (test PDFs) - zero installs, zero new system deps
- SERVICE (app/services/documents.py): extract_document(filename, bytes) → {engine, pages, chars, text, tables, meta}; engines by extension: pdf → pdfplumber (text per page + extract_tables lattice; broken page must not kill the doc), images (.png/.jpg/.jpeg/.bmp/.tif/.tiff/.webp) → pytesseract.image_to_string, .docx → python-docx paragraphs + tables (GOTCHA: row.cells returns _Cell objects - use c.text, first draft stringified the object), .xlsx → openpyxl read_only data_only (one table per sheet, sheets act as pages, first 5 sheets), .csv/.tsv → csv.reader (utf-8-sig → latin-1 fallback), .json → list-of-dicts becomes a table (header = union of keys in first-appearance order; nested values JSON-encoded), .txt/.md → text only. _clean_cell: None→"", 21.0→"21", datetime→str. Caps: text 200k chars, 50 tables, 10k rows/table, 25MB upload (aligned with datasets). best_table = row-heaviest with header+≥1 data row; table_to_items = header → dicts (empty header → col_N, dup → _2, pad short rows). coerce_items_dtypes: a column whose non-empty values ALL match ^-?(0|[1-9]\d*)$ → int, or float regex → float - "007"/mixed columns stay text, blanks preserved; this gives extracted datasets REAL dtypes (qty integer, amount number). Character-safety lesson from v27 ingest: never blind-cast

- DESIGN: the agent's wire protocol is JSON-directive-per-iteration, so raw token streaming would pour partial JSON into the UI; the right granularity is FRAME-level live trace: iteration, the model's raw reply, tool call, tool result, final answer - each landing on the SSE bus as it happens. The result reads like watching the agent think
- CONTEXT (engine/context.py): new emit field (async callable, v36) beside respond_channel; deliberately NOT in snapshot(); runner sets context.emit = self.emit for every run
- AGENT NODE (engine/nodes/agent.py): _emit_agent(context, event) - no-op when emit absent, swallows bus failures (a dead trace can never fail a run); emits agent_iteration / agent_reply (raw model reply capped 400) / agent_tool_call (args capped 300) / agent_tool_result (preview capped 240) / agent_answer. Full data still lands in the execution log via node output - frames are for the live UI
- STREAM ENDPOINT (api/chat.py): kind.startswith("agent_") branch translates bus events into SSE frames: event: agent, data: {phase, iteration, reply|tool|arguments|status|preview|answer, execution_id}; the v34 agent-only chat anchoring applies unchanged (agent-only workflows stream through the same POST /chat/{id}/stream)
- WS safety checked: ws.py forwards unknown event kinds verbatim and only special-cases execution_finished - editor live progress unaffected

Task ID: 48 (v37 feature wave - auth and multi-user)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: "proceed" after v36 closed (worklog scope note recommended user auth + multi-user as v37)

Work Log:
- Session start: summary stale a 14th time (claimed v36 not started); git truth: v36 already committed AND pushed (882c719, ls-remote verified), backend live at 1.36.0 - "proceed" therefore = v37 auth + multi-user (Task ID 48)
- DESIGN: two orthogonal layers. (1) Tokens always work: any request with a valid JWT scopes what it touches (resources stamped owner_id, lists filtered, foreign rows 404). (2) Enforcement is a mode: PY8N_REQUIRE_AUTH=true makes anonymous requests 401 on build/admin surfaces while machine + published surfaces stay open (webhooks, chat, app/dashboard runtimes, POST /datasets/query for embedded components, artifact content because <img> tags cannot send headers). Default OFF = legacy single-user behavior preserved 100%, so all 150 pre-existing tests ran unmodified
- CRYPTO: zero new dependencies - PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 passwords (240k iters, 16-byte salt, "pbkdf2_sha256$iters$salt$hash" format) + hand-rolled HS256 JWT (stdlib hmac/base64, exp-checked, compare_digest); signing secret auto-created 64-hex at data/.jwt.key (fernet key pattern)
- MODELS: users table (email unique, name, password_hash, role admin|member, created_at) + owner_id (nullable, indexed) on workflows, datasets, folders, credentials, env_variables, apps, dashboards; init_db adds the 7 columns to pre-existing dev DBs (create_all does not); executions/artifacts scoped transitively via workflow_id
- API (api/auth.py): GET /auth/status {require_auth, has_users, version} anonymous probe; POST /auth/register (409 dup, 422 short password via Field, first user -> admin + claims orphans); POST /auth/login (generic 401); GET /auth/me (401 without valid token)
- CLAIM ON FIRST REGISTER: claim_orphans() UPDATEs all 7 tables setting owner_id = first user WHERE NULL - an existing install flipping auth on later keeps its data under a real owner
- ENFORCEMENT WIRING (main.py): ENFORCED = [Depends(enforce_auth)] passed per include_router for 16 routers; exempt: health, auth, webhooks, chat, ws, debug _spawn; auth.is_public_path() checks prefixes + suffixes (/query, /runtime, /form, /form-submit, /records, /content) so runtime endpoints bypass the gate; health response grew require_auth field
- SCOPING PATCHES (12 routers): shared helpers in auth.py (get_optional_user, enforce_auth, own_or_404, scope_rows, visible_workflow_ids, claim_orphans); workflows 13 id-endpoints guarded via one replace_all on the db.get/404 block + list filter + create/import/duplicate stamping + get_version needed a manual wf load (it queried versions directly); datasets/folders/credentials/env_vars/apps/dashboards list+create+id endpoints; apps/dashboards runtime + form + records endpoints deliberately left public; executions list filters workflow_id.in_(visible), get/delete/rerun resolve the workflow and own_or_404; agents/schedules/insights filter by visible workflow ids; templates/use + documents/to-dataset stamp the created row; artifacts list keeps workflow_id NULL or visible
- GOTCHA (live-found): WorkflowOut response_model silently stripped owner_id (GET returned no owner field -> smoke assertion failed) - added owner_id to the schema; GOTCHA #2: tests hit the live dev sqlite where lifespan never runs under ASGITransport, so the new users table + owner_id columns must be migrated manually (python -c init_db) or 130 tests fail with "no such column"; GOTCHA #3: auth tests wipe users + reset owner stamps around themselves (delete(User) + update(*).values(owner_id=None)) to be repeatable against the live DB; GOTCHA #4: register password Field(min_length=8) rejects at pydantic layer with 422, not 400
- FRONTEND: stores/auth.ts (Pinia: token in localStorage py8n.token, user, status probe one-shot cached, boot() restores + validates via /auth/me, login/register/logout; builds gateway-mode URLs directly - must not import useApi, cycle); composables/useApi.ts request() now attaches Authorization from the store and onResponseError 401 + requireAuth -> clearSession + navigateTo(/login); middleware/auth.global.ts (client) public paths /login, /run/, /d/, /f/ skip; else boot() + enforced mode demands token + validated user; pages/login.vue (plain layout, Py8nLogo, auto-detects has_users=false -> "Create the owner account" copy, register vs signin modes, error bubble, redirect param); AppSidebar.vue user chip (initial, name/email, role, Sign out) above quick search, footer v1.37
- VERSION: 1.37.0 (config.py, footer, strict pin moved to test_v37, v36 relaxed to >= 1.36.0); docker-compose api gains documented PY8N_REQUIRE_AUTH knob; README gained "Auth & multi-user (v37)" section
- TESTS test_v37_features.py (5, suite 155/155): health pin 1.37.0 + require_auth false; register/login/me roundtrip (me resolves, fresh login token works, wrong password 401, no token 401, forged jwt 401, dup 409, short password 422, status probe coherent); scoping between two users (A creates -> B list hides it, B GET 404, B run 404, anonymous still sees it in open mode); enforced mode (settings.require_auth flipped in-test + restored: anonymous /workflows 401, /datasets 401, token 200, health/auth/status public, /datasets/query public, forged token 401); first register claims orphans (deterministic: wipe users first, orphan workflow, register -> role admin, claimed.workflows >= 1, row.owner_id == user.id)

PBKDF2 passwords + stdlib HS256 JWTs, first-user-claims-the-estate bootstrap, owner_id scoping across every core resource, and an opt-in enforcement mode (PY8N_REQUIRE_AUTH) that keeps webhooks, chat and published runtimes public while the build surface requires sign-in; default open mode preserves legacy behavior 100% (150 old tests untouched); 155/155 pytest + full smoke v27 -> v37 + enforced-mode browser E2E, backend live at 1.37.0 - next candidates: node resilience pack (retry/timeout/fallback + queue view), exportable template packs, dataset row previews in the agent trace, API keys for machine access

Task ID: 49 (v38 feature wave - node resilience pack + live queue)
Agent: main (Super Z)

Work Log:
- Session start: summary stale a 15th time (claimed v36 not started); git truth: v36 (882c719) AND v37 (c960ebe) both committed AND pushed, backend down + duckdb wiped (sandbox reset) - reinstalled duckdb 1.5.5, so "proceed" = v38 node resilience pack (Task ID 49)
- FOUNDATION FOUND: the engine already had n8n-parity retry (retry_on_fail/max_retries/retry_wait_ms) and continue_on_fail in NodeSettings + _run_node, with a ConfigPanel section; v38 adds the genuinely missing knobs
- TIMEOUT (schema.py NodeSettings): timeout_ms (0 = off, cap 600000); runner wraps EVERY attempt in asyncio.wait_for - a timeout is an ordinary failure so retry_on_fail applies to it; error text "timed out after Xs" (uses :g formatting); catches asyncio.TimeoutError BEFORE the generic Exception clause
- RETRY VISIBILITY: new node_retry event emitted before each retry attempt (attempt, max_attempts, last_error) - WS forwards unknown kinds verbatim and the py8n store's handleEvent only special-cases known events, so purely additive
- FALLBACK: fallback_enabled + fallback_value (Any) in NodeSettings; on final failure the configured value replaces the failure on the main handle and the flow keeps going; takes precedence over continue_on_fail; _record gained a fallback flag -> run record fallback_used=True (and continued_on_fail suppressed when fallback), status stays "error" on the node while the run succeeds - the error is still recorded
- EXECUTOR PROGRESS MAP: _live_progress dict fed by the emit wrapper (node_started -> current_node, node_finished -> nodes_done++, execution_finished -> status); registered before runner.run with nodes_total = len(graph.nodes), popped in the finally; loop batches count through the same emit so done can exceed total (clamped in the API)
- QUEUE ENDPOINT (api/executions.py): GET /executions/queue declared BEFORE /{execution_id} (route capture); merges DB running/waiting rows with the in-memory map; per item: workflow_name (batch-joined), trigger_type, started_at, live duration_ms for running rows, nodes_done/total (clamped), current_node; v37 scoping via visible_workflow_ids preserved; GOTCHA: sqlite started_at is naive - use datetime.utcnow(), mixing with now(timezone.utc) raises "can't subtract offset-naive and offset-aware datetimes"
- FRONTEND: types/node.ts NodeSettings += timeout_ms/fallback_enabled/fallback_value + new QueueItem; ConfigPanel On-fail section grew a Timeout input and a Fallback value toggle + JSON textarea (JSON.parse with raw-string fallback so nothing typed is dropped); pages/workflows/[id].vue default settings object extended; pages/executions/index.vue: queue ref + refreshQueue on a dedicated 2s timer, QueueItem cards (amber border when running, progress bar = min(100, done/total), current-node line, waiting copy, Cancel button reusing store.cancelExecution); queue section hidden when empty
- VERSION: 1.38.0 (config.py, footer v1.38 - 37 node types, strict pin moved to test_v38, v37 relaxed to >= 1.37.0)
- TESTS test_v38_features.py (5, suite 160/160): health pin; settings roundtrip (timeout/fallback fields survive create+read); timeout+retries (Delay 5s capped at 250ms with 1 retry -> error, attempts=2, "timed out after 0.25s", whole run < 4s proving the cap cut in); fallback feeds downstream (code node raises, fallback {"answer": 42} -> run success, node error + fallback_used, echo node received the fallback payload); queue endpoint (dispatch a 4s delay run -> item running with nodes_total=2, current_node Delay -> cancel -> run cancelled + leaves the queue). GOTCHA: the sandboxed code node has no ValueError in SAFE_BUILTINS - use "result = 1 / 0" (C-level ZeroDivisionError)
- SMOKE v38 section (live 1.38.0): fallback run (boom errors, flow delivers {"kept": True}) -> timeout run (5s delay cut at 250ms, < 4s) -> queue (4s delay caught running with current_node Delay, cancel -> leaves queue); full smoke ALL PASS v27 -> v38
- E2E (browser): editor On-fail settings shows all four knobs; fallback toggled ON + {"kept": true} typed into the JSON textarea; Save persisted the settings verbatim (verified via API: fallback_enabled true, fallback_value {"kept": true}); /executions queue strip showed the live card (MANUAL - 7.70s, progress bar, "running Slow Step", Cancel) and Cancel from the card cancelled the run (API: cancelled) and cleared the strip; zero console/page errors. GOTCHA: the editor's executions drawer covers the config panel bottom - set viewport 1280x1800 to capture the settings section
- Screenshots: download/e2e-v38-{resilience-settings,queue-live,queue-after-cancel,final}.png (download/ was wiped by the sandbox reset - recreated)

- CRITICAL /_spawn RCE backdoor: route removed from is_public_path, triple-gated (PY8N_DEBUG now defaults false + PY8N_SPAWN_ENABLED default false + per-boot random secrets.token_urlsafe(32) token when PY8N_SPAWN_TOKEN unset), 404 while disabled, compare_digest authz, cmd length cap, include_in_schema=False (absent from OpenAPI); the committed static token is gone from main.py + auth.py
- CRITICAL engine/sandbox.py (new): all three exec sites (code node, python_transform, agent code tool) now run through an AST guard (no underscore-prefixed attributes anywhere - dunders AND privates, no reflective builtins eval/exec/compile/getattr/open/__import__/..., allowlisted imports only, from-import dunder bindings rejected because they bypass proxies via sys.modules, no pickle surface, no dunder definitions), ModuleProxy exposure (public attrs only, enforced in __getattribute__ so the proxy's own slots are hidden), deepcopy_state (snippets can no longer mutate engine state by reference), run_bounded via anyio.to_thread + CapacityLimiter(4) with hard asyncio timeout (daemon workers - a runaway snippet can no longer hang interpreter shutdown, which ThreadPoolExecutor non-daemon threads did); sandboxed __import__ hooks in logic.py + datascience.py return PROXIED modules so explicit imports cannot rebind raw modules over the proxy; GOTCHA found during testing: DS_BUILTINS advertises sklearn via an import hook the AST guard must mirror (guard extra_roots={"sklearn"}), and the old _IMPORT_ERROR text promised sklearn to python_transform users while the map never had it (text corrected)
- CRITICAL run_sql: layered read-only gate (validate_readonly_sql: single SELECT/WITH statement, quote-aware comment stripping, semicolon separator detection, whole-word rejection of COPY/ATTACH/INSTALL/LOAD/PRAGMA/SET/CREATE/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/DROP/ALTER/CALL/EXPORT/IMPORT), fetchmany(max_sql_rows+1) row cap with truncated flag, owner-scoped views (only the caller's + unclaimed datasets are registered); agent dataset tool passes the run's owner too
- OWNER THREADING: GraphRunner(owner_id=) -> ExecutionContext.owner_id -> nodes scope every owner-capable service call (get_dataset, run_sql, decrypt_credential in http/llm/integrations/agent, load/append agent memory via _scoped_key, load_env_map, subflow GraphRunner); executor passes workflow.owner_id at both GraphRunner call sites (run + resume); legacy owner_id=None keeps all-visible semantics everywhere
- API hardening: WS auth (enforced mode requires JWT via ?token=, legacy anonymous unchanged), webhook body cap 413 with streaming guard + sensitive-header redaction (exact names + token/secret/password/key substrings, v4 40-header valve kept), _ratelimit.py sliding-window limiter on auth/webhook/chat (429 + Retry-After, env kill switch, monkeypatchable OVERRIDES + reset_all), executions resume/cancel ownership-checked, registries/packs fetches http(s)-only + streaming byte cap -> clean 502, keys route hygiene verified
- Secrets: PBKDF2 hash/verify offloaded with asyncio.to_thread (register/login no longer block the loop for ~100ms+), JWT secret file created 0o600 via os.open + chmod repair on read, Fernet key same treatment
- Reliability: executor _fail_execution safety net (any crash finalizes the row as error - no zombie running rows), celery dispatch pre-creates the running row, error-workflow handler failures isolated; retention purge excludes waiting/paused executions and sweeps orphaned artifact files; rules engine formula length cap + hardened AST allowlist
- llm-bridge binds 127.0.0.1 only (Bun.serve default was 0.0.0.0 with a dev /_spawn endpoint); backend spawn token contract unchanged for the platform's own bootstrap
- FRONTEND (10 files): apps builder config edits actually persist (save sends full config incl. rules, bindDataset re-saves, publish aborts when pre-save failed), run/[slug] edit/delete by stable raw index instead of search-filtered position, uploads via authenticated api.upload (documents + datasets), WS url + chat SSE carry the JWT, crypto.randomUUID ids for app components/rules/edges/paste, WS close-before-reconnect, listener + timer cleanup on unmount
- requirements.txt: anyio now a direct dep (sandbox pool), python-multipart + data/doc stack previously undeclared added

- TESTS: 190 -> 241 passing (test_sandbox_hardening.py 33: 17 escape payloads blocked through both guard surfaces + end-to-end CodeNode runs, proxy slot-leak regression test, deepcopy isolation, timeout, per-boot spawn-token randomness via subprocess boots + in-process 401/200 gating; test_audit_api_hardening.py + test_audit_services_hardening.py from the parallel agents); GOTCHA: ThreadPoolExecutor timeout tests hang pytest at exit on 3.9+ (non-daemon threads joined at interpreter shutdown) - the anyio daemon-thread pivot is a PRODUCTION fix, not just a test fix

- NEW NODES (8, all pandas-backed with the standard items-in/items-out wire format so they compose with everything):
  * join - true pandas.merge between Input A (main) and Input B (secondary): inner/left/right/outer/ANTI, m:N-safe (unlike compare_datasets' first-match), dot-path keys JSON-canonicalized into temp key columns, colliding B columns get suffix_right, merge indicator drives matched/left_only/right_only counts, empty-side short-circuits keep semantics correct where pandas would crash
  * pivot (pivot_table rows->matrix, 8 agg funcs, count fills 0) + unpivot (melt back to tidy with var_name/value_name)
  * cast_columns - per-column casting to integer|number|boolean|text|datetime with optional strptime format, on_error coerce (Int64 nullable / null) or raise with column-named errors
  * handle_nulls - drop (subset-capable) or fill with zero|empty|mean|median|mode|constant
  * data_quality - expectation checks not_null|unique|range|non_negative|allowed_values|regex|min_rows|max_rows|SCHEMA (dtype drift + missing columns), per-check failed counts + sample bad rows, on_fail warn (structured report for IF-routing) or error (fails the run - quality gates pipelines)
  * analyze - six modes: descriptive (mean/std/quartiles), correlation (pearson matrix, 15-col cap), outliers (IQR multiplier or z-score cutoff + bounds + sample rows), distribution (bins via pd.cut), value_counts (with pct), trend (timestamp resample hour/day/week/month x count/sum/avg/median + period-over-period growth)
  * dataset_export - dataset -> artifact (csv utf-8-sig for Excel / xlsx via openpyxl / json / parquet verbatim) with 200k-row cap; artifacts EXT_BY_TYPE learned csv/xlsx/parquet extensions
- SummarizeNode deepened (backward-compatible): count_distinct, median, std, first, last, concat (cap 100) + HAVING clauses on aggregate labels (> >= < <= == !=) + sort_by/sort_dir + limit
- dataset_write gains mode=upsert backed by services.datasets.upsert_rows (composite str-tuple keys, existing rows with matching keys REPLACED, new keys appended, written/updated/inserted counts, versioned source=upsert so it is undoable); key_columns required, validated against incoming items
- profile_df v2: backward-compatible keys preserved (v27 test pins them) + null_pct/unique_pct, std/median/q25/q75, zeros/negatives, IQR outlier bounds + count + sample rows, text length stats, DATETIME SHAPE DETECTION on text columns (>=90% parse rate -> parsed_as_datetime + span_days - JSON ingestion stores ISO timestamps as text), dataset-level duplicate_rows/completeness_pct/constant_columns/id_like_columns + pearson correlation matrix (15-col cap)
- GET /datasets/{id}/export?fmt= (owner-scoped direct download with Content-Disposition; 400 on bad format); composables/useApi.ts gains download() (Bearer-aware blob fetch + object-URL anchor - raw <a href> cannot carry the JWT in enforced mode)
- Dataset page: export dropdown (4 formats), dataset-level profile strip (completeness/dups/constant/correlations), per-column quantiles + IQR outlier warnings + datetime spans + correlation chips
- VERSION: 1.45.0 (config.py + AppSidebar; v44 health pin relaxed to >=)

Task ID: 62 (v50)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: v50 - the Data OS foundation: data contracts, dataset health, catalog, incremental ingestion, dataset triggers, connectors.

Work Log:
- DATA CONTRACTS: DatasetContract model (one active per dataset; columns_json = name/dtype/nullable/allowed; on_violation warn|error; versioned on replace), services/contracts.py (definition validation, castability-based checking - "7" IS an integer, "abc" is not; rules missing_column/not_null/dtype/allowed), enforced in the dataset_write node (error = NodeExecutionError before anything lands; warn = violations ride on the output) AND the rows API (422, write refused), PUT/GET/DELETE /datasets/{id}/contract + POST .../contract/check (lint candidate rows or the CURRENT contents, 400 when no contract).
- DATASET HEALTH: services/health.py computes a derived report - freshness (version timeline age, fresh/hours/stale/cold tiers), volume (rows vs previous version, delta + pct), schema (contract present/ok/violations), quality (profile-based completeness, null rate, dup rows), one weighted 0-100 score with healthy/degraded/unhealthy status; GET /datasets/{id}/health. Nothing stored = can never drift.
- DATA CATALOG: services/catalog.py + GET /catalog?q=&tag= - per-dataset cards assembling identity (name/description/owner/tags), shape (rows/cols/versions), freshness tier, contract badge, PRODUCERS (distinct workflows from version lineage) and CONSUMERS (scan of active workflow graphs for dataset_read/dataset_write/sql_query/dataset_export nodes referencing the view name); entries DERIVED, never stored. New pages/catalog/index.vue (cards grid, search, tag filter, health dots, producer/consumer chips) + sidebar entry.
- INCREMENTAL INGESTION: IngestionState model (per dataset_id+key cursor; key names the pipeline), services/ingestion.py (watermark_gt numeric-else-ISO/string compare, filter_incremental, advance), dataset_write gains mode=incremental + watermark_column + ingestion_key - only rows strictly beyond the stored checkpoint land (the CDC pattern: WHERE last_updated > checkpoint without source-side memory); unwatermarked rows skipped and counted; node output carries rows_in/skipped/checkpoint_before/checkpoint_after; GET/DELETE /datasets/{id}/ingestion-states[/key] (inventory + reset).
- DATASET TRIGGERS (data-DAG orchestration): new dataset_trigger node (watches a dataset, poll_seconds >= 30); scheduler._poll_dataset_trigger uses an IngestionState cursor keyed trigger:{node_id} - first poll ARMS without firing (no activation stampede), later polls fire exactly once per new version (cursor advances BEFORE dispatch, so a slow run never double-fires); resync_workflow_jobs registers interval jobs for dataset_trigger nodes alongside schedule nodes; trigger payload exposes dataset/dataset_id/version/row_count/source; "don't run customer_metrics until clean_customers succeeds" is now this trigger + downstream nodes.
- CONNECTORS: engine/nodes/connectors.py - db_source (sqlite/postgres/mysql via SQLAlchemy; SELECT-only keyword guard; optional database-type credential builds the URL host/port/user/password/database; bare sqlite paths URL-ified) and s3_source (S3/MinIO/any S3-compatible via boto3; endpoint_url for self-hosted; csv/xlsx/json/parquet sniffing; aws-type credential or env creds); registry now 52 node types; requirements.txt grows psycopg2-binary/pymysql/boto3.
- UI: dataset page grows a live HEALTH STRIP (status word + score + fresh/stale + schema-valid + row delta vs prev version + quality %) and a collapsible DATA CONTRACT editor (column rows with dtype select/nullable/allowed-values, warn|error mode, save/remove, "Check current data" lint with per-rule violation report); catalog page + sidebar.
- Tests: tests/test_v50_features.py (11 tests: definitions pin, contract CRUD, check endpoint, rows-API enforcement, engine enforcement incl. warn-mode violations on output, health incl. volume delta + contract degradation, catalog incl. producers/consumers + filters, incremental incl. replay idempotency + reset + missing-watermark error, dataset trigger arm/fire/no-double-fire + downstream run + payload, db_source sqlite + read-only guard + missing-table error, s3_source fake-client csv + URI validation); v27/v49 definition pins relaxed to the new wave; 286 passed + 7 deliberate skips, Nuxt build green.
- Live smoke (scripts/smoke_v50_live.py, real uvicorn :8811): 13 checks - contract 422 + castable pass, health healthy/96.3, catalog card, incremental checkpoint 7->9 with idempotent replay, state inventory, db_source reading the instance's own sqlite, trigger arm/fire/no-double-fire with a downstream write landing.

GET /storage (api/storage.py) reports kind/bucket/prefix/endpoint + live ping. Datasets page header grows a storage badge (LOCAL / S3 / MinIO / GCS, green on ping pass).
- DATA-DAG POLICIES: workflows gain policy_json (validated by schemas.validate_execution_policy: retries 0-5, backoff_ms 0-60k, backoff_multiplier 1-10, backoff_max_ms 0-300k, timeout_seconds 0-3600, retry_on all|transient; create accepts it, update is tri-state omitted=untouched/{}=clear/object=replace; WorkflowOut exposes it via a before-validator mapping policy_json -> policy). runner._run_node resolves the effective policy per node (node settings retry_on_fail/timeout_ms WIN, then workflow policy, then defaults), retries with EXPONENTIAL BACKOFF (wait = backoff_ms * multiplier^(n-1) capped at backoff_max_ms; node_retry events carry wait_ms), enforces the policy timeout per attempt, and retry_on=transient stops the loop on PERMANENT errors - _is_permanent_error walks the exception CAUSE chain (NodeExecutionError wrapping a data-contract ContractViolation/ValueError is permanent; timeout/connection/5xx/throttling messages are transient) so contract breaches fail on attempt 1 while a throttled S3 call retries. node_runs records carry the policy each node ran under (source node|workflow|node-default, retries, backoff, timeout, retry_on) + final_attempt reflects early stops (attempts=1 + "permanent error, retries skipped" in the error). executor passes workflow.policy_json into GraphRunner on both the fresh-run and resume paths. Workflow settings modal grows a Data-DAG execution policy section (on/off toggle, retries/backoff/backoff-x/timeout fields, retry_on select).
- DASHBOARD SHARE AUDIT (parity with apps): DashboardAuditEvent model (dashboard_id, action view_dashboard|access, outcome allowed|denied, detail) capped at the newest 500 per board; api/dashboards._audit_share_access logs ONLY protected boards (share_token set; force=True for rejections), owns its commit and never raises - the runtime endpoint audits denied attempts (missing/invalid token) BEFORE the 403 and allowed renders after the gate passes; GET /dashboards/{id}/share/audit returns {dashboard_id, protected, total, events[]} (owner-only, newest first, limit param); dashboards share dialog shows a collapsible "Recent share activity" feed (dot color by outcome, denial details, time-ago) mirroring the apps dialog.

Task ID: 65 (v79 - recording/transcription archives, the browser meeting client, the SMS auto-answer)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: "proceed with this: recording/transcription archives for video meetings, the browser meeting client UI in the app itself, and SMS inbound auto-answer (reply '1' to leave the queue)."

Work Log:
- RECORDING/TRANSCRIPTION ARCHIVES (services/voice_recordings.py + the VoiceMeetingRecording model): a meeting archives what py8n actually CARRIES, honestly - one recording pass per room at a time (state recording -> stopped; a duplicate start refuses loudly), the room is TOLD on every leg's timeline (recording.started / recording.stopped joined EVENT_KINDS), the WORDS (the derived merged transcript + the room chat log) are snapshotted at stop time into REAL artifacts (a markdown transcript with speakers/confidences/chat + a json chat log, kind=recording), and each captured WEB leg's utterance audio (the media websocket's own speech.ended PCM) accumulates in an IN-PROCESS capture registry (start_capture/append_utterance/pop_capture; honest ceilings: MAX_SESSIONS_PER_RECORDING, MAX_PCM_BYTES_PER_SESSION skipped-and-counted, a mid-stream sample-rate change refused) and is written at stop as a REAL RIFF/WAV artifact (stdlib wave, mono, the stream's own sample rate); phone legs ride the carrier's media plane - their audio is honestly absent, said per leg in the archive's audio blocks; the row is the handle, the artifacts are the content (GET ?include=true embeds the words: markdown as text, chat as json); hooks: join_participant starts capture for legs joining a RECORDED room (the join response carries recording.capturing), end_meeting stops the active recording automatically via stop_active_for_meeting (a failed archive lands on the row as state=failed, never failing the room's end; the end response carries the recording block); API: POST/GET /voice/meetings/{id}/recordings, POST .../recordings/{rid}/stop, GET /voice/recordings (owner-wide shelf), GET /voice/recordings/{rid}?include=true; the media websocket loop feeds append_utterance at every closed utterance (capture happens BEFORE ASR resolution - a room records even with no ASR engine registered)
- THE BROWSER MEETING CLIENT (pages/meetings/index.vue + pages/meetings/[id].vue + useApi.mediaUrl()): the room in the app itself - the /meetings shelf (rooms + create + end + the archive shelf with transcript/chat downloads) and the room client: join as a WEB leg (label -> POST /meetings/{id}/join, the participant/session pair parked in sessionStorage so a refresh re-attaches), the mic streams linear16 16k over the session's media websocket (AudioContext ScriptProcessor -> downsample -> base64 media frames; start frame carries customParameters), the platform's pushes land live (chat / queue_position banner / audio frames played as WAV / turn updates / video_signal), camera + screen share publish as first-class v78 tracks with the PUBLISHER OFFERING to every other joined leg (glare-free mesh: one offer direction, ICE candidates relayed through video_signal both ways, pending-candidate queue before the remote description lands), hand-raise/lower + call-next, the live transcript (derived, polled), recording start/stop + the archive list with downloads, the leave door (hangup event), a 5s room poll; a Meetings sidebar entry next to Channels (the operator console stays where it is)
- THE SMS AUTO-ANSWER (voice_waiting.try_auto_answer riding channel_endpoints.receive_webhook BEFORE the interaction ingest): the backchannel LISTENS - a text arriving from a sender who is WAITING in a queue whose SMS backchannel rides THIS endpoint (owner-scoped like sms_update_pass) with sms.auto_answer enabled and the text matching the keyword ("1", case/space-insensitive) is an answer to the QUEUE'S OFFER, not a turn for the agent: action=callback composes the v78 callback (request_callback: the place is kept, the hold ends reason=callback, the target is booked on the composed campaign), action=abandon releases the call honestly (leave_queue: back to in_progress, nobody silently dropped), the confirmation text goes back through the SAME endpoint (DEFAULT_AUTO_REPLY_CALLBACK/ABANDON templates with {queue_name} {position} {depth} {waited_seconds} {keyword}, or the queue's own reply_template - all validated at CONFIG WRITE via waiting_config, an invalid action/placeholder refuses on queue create), every step lands in the entry's backchannel log (inbound_reply / auto_answer_reply / auto_answer_refused, the existing MAX_SMS_HISTORY cap) and on the session timeline as queue.sms; non-keyword text and senders who are not waiting ride the normal conversation path (handled without the auto_answer flag, conversation_id present); a refused action is a RECORD (the webhook still 200s, the entry stays waiting) - deliberately NO rollback in the refusal path (the request session still owes the webhook's writes; a rollback would expire the endpoint/session rows and trip MissingGreenlet - found live and documented in the code); receive_webhook commits at the end so the endpoint counters persist even when every message was intercepted
- TESTS: tests/test_v79_features.py (6 tests): recording units (RIFF/WAV bytes, capture lifecycle, rate-change + ceiling refusals), the archive E2E (start/duplicate-refuse, recording.started on the legs, seeded asr.final/tts.started + 2 chat lines, a REAL utterance through the REAL media websocket via the v70 ASGI _WSClient pattern closing on speech.ended, stop -> exact counts + per-leg coverage (1 captured / 1 honestly absent) + markdown/json/WAV artifacts verified byte-level + include=true + the shelf lists + stop-twice refuse + cross-owner 404), the room lifecycle (join-into-recording captures from arrival, end auto-stops with stop_reason=meeting_ended, a dead meeting refuses new recordings), the auto-answer callback E2E (signed generic_sms webhook "1" -> the entry left via the composed campaign, reason=callback, campaign target booked, the default template rendered, honest skipped delivery, non-keyword + non-waiting senders ride the normal path), abandon + guards (config-write refusals for bad action/template, abandon releases to in_progress, callback-action on a callback-less queue refused honestly as a record with the entry untouched, auto-answer-disabled line treats "1" as a normal message), the 1.79.0 version pin; v76/v77/v78 strict pins relaxed to >=
- Fixed along the way: active_recording_for_meeting returned an un-awaited coroutine (found by the join hook test), the include-reader tried to JSON-parse the markdown transcript (now reads each artifact as itself), the em/en-dash sweep surfaced pre-existing offenders in pages/channels/index.vue (swept - the v35 guard is green again)
- Live smoke (scripts/smoke_v79_live.py, real uvicorn :8207): 2 checks - the recording archive E2E (real media websocket utterance -> RIFF/WAV artifact verified, join-into-recording, auto-stop on end, markdown/chat artifacts downloaded), the SMS auto-answer through a SIGNED webhook ("1" -> callback composition + honest skipped confirmation + campaign target; non-keyword rides the conversation layer)
- VERSION: 1.79.0 (config.py + sidebar v1.79)
- TESTS RUN: 437 passed + 7 deliberate skips (431 -> 437), Nuxt production build green

Stage Summary:
- Py8n v1.79.0: the meeting has a MEMORY (words + web-leg audio as real artifacts, archived the moment the pass stops, honest per leg about what py8n never carried), a FRONT DOOR (join the room from the app - mic, camera, screen, chat, floor, recordings), and the waiting line gained a text door (reply "1" and the queue itself ends the hold - into the callback composition or out of the line, confirmed by text, recorded at every step). - THE MACHINE (models + services/business_processes.py, new): BusinessProcess (the definition - states, initial, named transitions; validate_definition refuses loudly: empty/duplicate states, initial not in states, transitions referencing unknown states, duplicates, a machine with no moves) + BusinessProcessInstance (the tracked entity that REMEMBERS: ref = the external key the business already uses, context = the running memory the team and agents read and write, state + entered_state_at = the present, due_at = the SLA promise, ended_at set on entering a TERMINAL state - terminal = no outgoing transitions, derived never stored) + BusinessProcessTransitionLog (every advance on the record - the deliberate stored exception: a business's history IS the product; from_state nullable for the 'started' row).
- THE MOVES: start_instance (initial state, context seeded, optional SLA), advance_instance (by transition NAME or by target to_state; anything the machine does not allow is a loud refusal NAMING the allowed moves; the context patch MERGES into the running memory with fresh-dict discipline - the JSON column is never mutated in place; due_at can be re-armed per move), advance from a terminal state refuses (the machine has no outgoing moves - the journey is complete), instance_out derives age_in_state_seconds + is_terminal + is_stuck (due_at passed while still open), journey readable on its own.
- THE BUSINESS MOVING IS OBSERVABLE: every accepted advance emits business.state_changed through the v80 door (source "business" added to EVENT_SOURCES, /events/contracts grew the business.* catalog) with from/to/transition/ref in the payload and correlation_id = the instance id - one correlation thread per journey; event-trigger workflows (business.state_changed or business.*) dispatch real executions with the event as payload: proposal_sent -> send the follow-up, negotiating > 7 days -> escalate. THE COMPOSITION IS THE POINT: business state machine + agents + workflows + data + interactions.
- THE PROCESS, MEASURED (all derived): process_analytics = instances by state, open vs terminal, stuck instances (SLA), mean time in state per state derived from the log's own timestamps (SQLite-naive datetimes normalized through _aware - found live as a TypeError), advance counts per transition.
- THE ESTATE: a process binds to a system as a first-class component - "process" added to py8n_systems COMPONENT_KINDS + KIND_TABLES (attach validates against the live table; the system detail groups it by name).
- API (api/processes.py): POST/GET /processes, GET /processes/{id} + /analytics, POST /processes/{id}/instances (start), GET instances (+state filter), GET instance (with the journey), POST advance {transition?|to_state?, note, context_patch, due_in_seconds?}. Registered with ENFORCED.

Loud refusals: an empty/non-object patch, and ANY write under the reserved 'escalations' key (the door's episode bookkeeping is not the agents' scratch space). Terminal entities still accept annotations (facts keep landing after the journey completes). BusinessAnnotateNode resolves by instance_id or ref-within-process like business_advance, on_missing=skip keeps reactive runs green, returns the merged context so the agent reads after it writes.

Admission is honest: the door must have knocked for THIS stint (episode book count >= 1 matching the state, or an escalation marker on the log this stint - covering no-policy machines too); acknowledging silence refuses loud. record_episode preserves an ack already in the episode (race-safety); a state change starts a fresh episode and the door may knock again. escalate_stuck surfaces acknowledged holds with acked_by in both the policy and no-policy paths. BusinessAckNode (the handler's own '1' text acks through a workflow reacting to sms.received).

Task ID: 74 (v89 - the digest + the snooze + the cross-operator handoff)
Agent: main (Super Z)
Task: "proceed with : an escalation digest (one summary per day instead of N knocks), snooze semantics on acks (re-knock after N hours), and cross-operator journeys (a won deal opening an onboarding case)." - exactly the three next dimensions of the door and the departments, one round.

Work Log:
- THE SNOOZE (services/escalations.py + business_processes.py + api/processes.py + engine/nodes/business.py): snooze_hours on the ack turns the v88 take into a LOAN - record_ack stamps ack.snooze_hours + ack.snooze_until (now + N hours) onto the episode bookkeeping, episode_gate holds on the acked branch ONLY until the snooze runs out (the snooze-expired episode falls through to the cadence + cap checks and the door RE-KNOCKS, the attempt count continuing inside the same episode: re-knock after N hours, as asked), re-acking replaces the loan (snoozing again extends it), and an ack WITHOUT a snooze still owns the rest of the state stint (v88 semantics untouched, backward compatible). The held list shows the loan (reason=acknowledged + snooze_until + snooze_remaining_seconds), the escalation_acknowledged journey row and the business.escalation_acknowledged event name it, the REST API ack body and the business_ack node both carry snooze_hours (the handler's own '1' reply snoozes for N hours through a workflow), negative snooze refuses loud (pydantic ge=0 + the service double-checks).
- THE DIGEST (services/escalations.py + business_processes.py): mode="digest" + digest_every_seconds on the escalation policy replaces the N knocks with ONE summary per window. digest_gate per stuck instance decides only WHO belongs in the summary (fresh episode -> the door writes the pending book {state, count: 0, first_at} NOW or the window would restart every tick and never elapse; acked - snooze and all - holds; past 1 + max_repeats appearances holds episode_complete; else candidate with pending_since = digest_last_at or first_at). The bucket (one per owner+process) is due when its OLDEST pending_since has waited digest_every_seconds; then deliver_digest renders ONE message (a line per item: title, ref, state, overdue minutes, digest number) + ONE business.escalation_digest event on the process (payload carries every item, the window, the target), through the owner's channel endpoint with the SAME honest-skip semantics as knocks (no channel/target/endpoint -> skipped, named, never silent); every listed item gets its book stamped (count = digest number, digest_last_at) and an 'escalation_digest' no-move journey row (PAPERWORK_TRANSITIONS, counted apart in analytics as 'digests'). The machine's own escalate move is deliberately NOT taken in digest mode (a self-loop per tick would re-arm the stints the digest bookkeeping rides on) and business.stuck fires ONCE per episode (fresh observation), not per tick - that is the whole point. Validation: mode in {knock, digest} (default knock), the two clocks refuse to ride together loudly (digest + repeat_every_seconds, or knock + a non-default digest_every_seconds), digest_every_seconds >= 60; the normalized policy carries mode + digest_every_seconds so the definition round-trips its own validation. describe_policy: "stuck -> daily digest over email (x4) -> ops@...". The door report grew 'digest': {sent: [...], pending: [{items, next_in_seconds}]}; the Finance operator's Invoice lifecycle policy became the daily-digest flagship (the v86 door walk moved to the procurement machine, same knock shape).
- CROSS-OPERATOR JOURNEYS (services/business_processes.py + operators.py): journeys ride the process definition - {on_state, open: {process, state?, title_template?, ref_template?, memory?}} - validated loudly at write time (fire-state must be a state of this machine, one journey per fire-state, open.process required, unknown keys refused, memory must be an object) and resolved by NAME at FIRE time (_resolve_process_ref: id or case-insensitive name, most recent wins, owner-scoped - operators install independently, so the target may arrive with a later install). advance_instance now fires the journeys for the state it landed on: the leg's ref/title render from the source's fields (broken/empty templates fall back to the source's own ref/title), an OPEN instance already carrying the ref on the target machine skips (never double-tracks - the escalate self-loop can re-fire a journey safely), a missing target or a refused start is an HONEST business.journey_skipped on the source's correlation thread (the advance itself still succeeds - a misconfigured handoff must not roll back a won deal), and success lands business.journey_opened (source identity + nested target) + journeys_opened on the advance response. The opened context carries the spec's memory plus the journey link (from_process/from_instance/from_ref/from_state/at); journeys fire on ADVANCES only - the opened leg starts its own life and nothing recurses through the start. process_out surfaces journeys; operator_detail shows them per machine ("on_state -> opens"). The _JOURNEYS pack table (applied at install beside the v87 policy table) wires the flagship: the Sales operator's Lead pipeline ships won -> Customer onboarding; the Operations operator ships the landing machine (Customer onboarding: kickoff -> provisioning -> training -> go_live -> handed_off, escalate self-loops on every working state, seeded from the new 'Onboarding cases' dataset at each row's stage, its own generated data on-ramp) - the handoff between two departments is a state change, not a meeting.
- EVENTS + API: the contracts catalog grew business.escalation_digest, business.journey_opened, business.journey_skipped; the ack endpoint gained snooze_hours.
- FRONTEND: /processes gained the journey chips on the machine header (won -> Customer onboarding, fuchsia), the snooze field on the ack form (hours; empty = owns the stint), and the digest-xN chip beside the escalated-xN chip on tracked instances; sidebar v1.89 (68 node types - no new nodes, the door grew, not the canvas).
- TESTS: tests/test_v89_features.py (5 tests) - the snooze (negative refusing 422, the loan's receipt on the book + journey + event, the held list showing the loan, the door re-knocking at attempt 2 past the 1h loan on the injected clock, the 2h re-ack holding again, the plain ack owning the stint with nothing sneaking past, the fresh episode knocking, the node snoozing); the digest (four loud validation refusals, the summary string, the fresh candidates pending with ZERO knocks, business.stuck once per item, the window elapsing into ONE escalation_digest event covering both items with the honest skip, the analytics counting digest receipts, an ack quieting one item so the next digest lists only the other at attempt 2, the cap holding both items after their appearances); the journeys (four validation refusals, the win opening the case with rendered title/ref/state, the linked context, business.journey_opened with the nested target, the ghost-target honest skip on the record, the never-double-track skip, all through the API); the operator story (Operations + Sales installs, the built Lead pipeline carrying the journey, a fresh lead walking reach_out -> ... -> win, the Customer onboarding case opening itself at kickoff ref = the lead's phone with the link + via memory, the seeded desk rows untouched beside it); version pin. Version pins v79-v88 moved to 1.89.0 per convention; the v86 install expectations updated for Operations (two machines, 5 workflows) and the v86 door walk moved from the (now digest-mode) finance machine to the procurement machine.
- Verified: 507 passed + 7 deliberate skips (502 -> 507, no regressions); Nuxt production build green; live smoke scripts/smoke_v89_live.py (real uvicorn :8218, no auth) 3 checks green - the snooze through the REAL door (knock -> the loan held with snooze_remaining_seconds), the digest window walking on the real clock into ONE summary (2 items, honest skip, the event), a fresh lead winning and the Customer onboarding case opening itself (kickoff, linked, on the event thread). 1.89.0 asserted.

- Committed and pushed to origin/main; mirror synced.
